### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Judges 13:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/13:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/13:11:1)


**אַחֲרֵי אִשְׁתּוֹ.** אַחַר עֲצָתָהּ:


**Behind his wife.** Following her advice.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Judges 13:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/13:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/13:12:1)


**מַה יִּהְיֶה מִשְׁפַּט הַנַּעַר וּמַעֲשֵׂהוּ.** (תרגום:) מָה חֲזִי לְמֶעֱבַד לְעוּלֵימָא וּמָה נַעֲבִיד לֵיהּ:


**What regulations must we follow with the boy? How shall we treat him?** What should appropriately be done with the boy, what must we do with him?³

footnotes:
³ Targum Yonasan. The question was not what must the boy do, but what must we do with him.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Judges 13:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/13:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/13:15:1)


**נַעְצְרָה נָּא אוֹתָךְ.** נֶאֱסֹף אוֹתְךָ אֶל הַבַּיִת:


**Allow us to detain you** as our house guest.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Judges 13:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/13:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/13:17:1)


**כִּי יָבֹא דְבָרְךָ.** אִם יָבֹא לָנוּ שְׁלִיחוּת דָּבָר מֵאִתְּךָ, מִשֶּׁנֵּדַע וְנַכִּיר אֶת שְׁמֶךָ:


**When your instructions reach us.** If we are contacted with instructions to fulfill a mission for you, and we know and recognize your name,


###### Rashi on Judges 13:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/13:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/13:17:2)


**וְכִבַּדְנוּךָ.** וְנַעֲשֶׂה מַה שֶּׁתִּשְׁלַח לָנוּ:


**We shall honor you** by fulfilling the mission.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Judges 13:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/13:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/13:18:1)


**וְהוּא פֶלִאי.** מְכֻסֶּה, תָּמִיד הוּא מִשְׁתַּנֶּה וְאֵין יָדוּעַ לְמָה מִשְׁתַּנֶּה הַיּוֹם (ספרי נשא מב):


**When it is mystical.** It is concealed. It changes constantly,⁴ so that its identity remains unknown on any given day.

footnotes:
⁴ According to the angel’s mission at that particular time. Cf. Rashi to Bereishis, 32:30.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Judges 13:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/13:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/13:23:1)


**וְכָעֵת.** הַזֹּאת:


**At this time [lit. at the time].** At this time.


###### Rashi on Judges 13:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/13:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/13:23:2)


**לֹא הִשְׁמִיעָנוּ כָּזֹאת.** לֹא הָיָה מַשְׁמִיעֵנוּ בְּשׂוֹרָה זוֹ אִם רְאוּיִים אָנוּ לָמוּת:


**He would not have apprised us in this way.** He would not have apprised us of these tidings⁵ if we deserved to die.

footnotes:
⁵ Concerning the birth of a son.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Judges 13:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/13:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/13:25:1)


**לְפַעֲמוֹ.** לָבֹא בְּקִרְבּוֹ לִפְעָמִים:


**Pulsating within him.** Visiting him intermittently.



## Chapter 14


### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Judges 14:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/14:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/14:4:1)


**כִּי תֹאֲנָה הוּא מְבַקֵּשׁ.** עֲלִילָה לְהִתְגָּרוֹת בָּם:


**As He sought a pretext.** An accusation with which to provoke a confrontation against them.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Judges 14:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/14:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/14:7:1)


**וַיְדַבֵּר לָאִשָּׁה.** עַל הָאִשָּׁה, דִּבֵּר לִקְרוֹבֶיהָ, כֵּן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן:


**And discussed [lit. spoke with] the woman.** He spoke of the woman¹ with her relatives. This is Targum Yonasan's rendition.

footnotes:
¹ Not “with” the woman.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Judges 14:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/14:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/14:8:1)


**עֲדַת דְּבוֹרִים.** (תרגום:) קִינָא דְדַבְרַיָּתָא, כְּנִיסַת דְּבוֹרִים:


**A colony of bees.** A beehive—a swarm of bees.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Judges 14:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/14:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/14:9:1)


**וַיִּרְדֵּהוּ.** לְשׁוֹן הַבְדָּלַת דִּבּוּק שֶׁל כָּל דָּבָר הַנִּדְבָּק, וְכֵן לְעִנְיַן פַּת הַנִּדְבֶּקֶת בַּתַּנּוּר, נִקְרֵאת הַבְדָּלָתוֹ רְדִיָּה, (שבת קיז ב): הָרוֹדֶה פַּת מִן הַתַּנּוּר, (בבא מציעא סד א): הָרוֹדֶה אֶת כַּוַּרְתּוֹ, עַל שֵׁם שֶׁהַדְּבַשׁ עָשׂוּי עַל יְדֵי חַלּוֹת שֶׁעוֹשֶׂה מְדֻבָּק בְּדָפְנֵי הַכְּלִי בְּכָל צְדָדִין מִדּוֹפֶן אֶל דּוֹפֶן, כְּמִדַּת עוֹגֶל הַכְּלִי, וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: וַיִּרְדֵּהוּ, וּנְסַחְיֵהּ:


**He dislodged it.** This connotes the separation of an attached object. Similarly, with regard to a loaf attached to an oven, separation is described as "dislodging"—² "If one dislodges a loaf from the oven,"³ "If one dislodges his hive,"⁴ as the honey is produced in wax combs attached to the walls of the hive in every area, extending from wall to wall along its circumference. Similarly, Targum Yonasan translates "He removed it."

footnotes:
² Shabbos, 117:b.
³ Shabbos, 95:a.
⁴ The honeycomb from the hive.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Judges 14:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/14:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/14:10:1)


**וַיֵּרֶד אָבִיהוּ אֶל הָאִשָּׁה.** (תרגום:) עַל עִסְקֵי אִתְּתָא:


**His father descended to discuss the woman.** With regard to the woman.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Judges 14:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/14:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/14:11:1)


**מֵרֵעִים.** חֲבֵרִים לְשׁוֹשְׁבִינוּת:


**Friends.** Companions who formed the retinue of wedding attendants.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Judges 14:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/14:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/14:15:1)


**וַיְהִי בַּיּוֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִי.** לִימוֹת הַשַּׁבָּת, וְלֹא שְׁבִיעִי לַמִּשְׁתֶּה, וְהוּא רְבִיעִי לִימֵי הַמִּשְׁתֶּה:


**It was on the seventh day** of the week, but not the seventh of the feast. It was, rather, the fourth of the feast days.


###### Rashi on Judges 14:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/14:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/14:15:2)


**הַלְיָרְשֵׁנוּ קְרָאתֶם לָנוּ הֲלֹא.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: הַלְמִסְכְּנוּתָנָא קְרִיתוֹן יָתָנָא הָלְכָא:


**Was it to impoverish us that you invited us here.** Targum Yonasan renders: Was it to make us poor⁵ that you called us here?⁶

footnotes:
⁵ לְיָרְשֵׁנוּ is not interpreted as “to inherit us,” but as “to make us poor.”
⁶ הֲלֹא is not interpreted as “Did you not? but as “here.”



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Judges 14:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/14:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/14:17:1)


**שִׁבְעַת הַיָּמִים.** הַנּוֹתָרִים מִשִּׁבְעַת הַיָּמִים, מֵרְבִיעִי וְאֵילָךְ:


**The seven days.** What remained of the seven days, after the fourth day.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Judges 14:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/14:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/14:18:1)


**בְּטֶרֶם יָבֹא הַחַרְסָה.** לִפְנֵי שְׁקִיעַת הַחַמָּה, שֶׁעֲדַיִן הָיָה לָהֶם שֵׁהוּת עַד הָעֶרֶב:


**Before sunset.** Before the sun descended, as they still had time until nightfall.


###### Rashi on Judges 14:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/14:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/14:18:2)


**חֲרַשְׁתֶּם בְּעֶגְלָתִי.** מָשָׁל הוּא (תרגום:) אִילּוּלְפוֹן בְּדַקְתּוּן בְּאִתְּתִי:


**Plowed with my calf.** This is allegorical—if you had not questioned my wife.⁷⁸

footnotes:
⁷ This is Targum Yonasan’s translation.
⁸ Who is cherished as a young calf is cherished. (Sanhedrin, 21:a.)



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Judges 14:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/14:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/14:19:1)


**רוּחַ ה׳.** (תרגום:) רוּחַ גְּבוּרָה מִן קֳדָם ה׳:


**The Divine spirit.** A spirit of power, Divinely inspired.⁹

footnotes:
⁹ This is Targum Yonasan’s translation.


###### Rashi on Judges 14:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/14:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/14:19:2)


**חֲלִיצוֹתָם.** מַלְבּוּשִׁים שֶׁחָלַץ מֵעֲלֵיהֶן:


**Their garments.** The garments which he had stripped from them.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Judges 14:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/14:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/14:20:1)


**לְמֵרֵעֵהוּ.** לְאֶחָד מִשּׁוֹשְׁבִינָיו נְתָנָהּ:


**To a Friend.** To one of the wedding attendants.



## Chapter 15


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Judges 15:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:3:1)


**נִקֵּיתִי הַפַּעַם.** בְּדִין אֶעֱשֶׂה לָהֶם הָרָעָה:


**At this time I am innocent.** I shall be justified in inflicting injury upon them.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Judges 15:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:4:1)


**וַיֶּפֶן.** אֶת הַזְּנָבוֹת זָנָב אֶל זָנָב. וַיִּפֶן, מַשְׁמַע הוּא עַצְמוֹ פָּנָה (שמות לב טו); וַיֶּפֶן, מַשְׁמַע הִפְנָה אֶת אֲחֵרִים, וְכֵן שִׁמּוּשׁ כָּל תֵּיבָה שֶׁפֹּעַל שֶׁלָּהּ בְּה״א; (שמות א כ): וַיִּרֶב הָעָם; (איכה ב ה): וַיֶּרֶב בְּבַת יְהוּדָה תַּאֲנִיָּה; (מלכים ב כה כא): וַיִּגֶל יְהוּדָה מֵעַל אַדְמָתוֹ, גָּלָה; (שם ב: יח יא): וַיֶּגֶל מֶלֶךְ אַשּׁוּר אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל אַשּׁוּרָה, הִגְלָה:


**Twisted.** The tails, each into the next. **וַיִפֶן** implies that he himself turned, **וַיֶפֶן** that he turned, or twisted, others. This usage applies with every word whose verbal root ends with **ה**.¹ "The people increased;"² "He increased, in the people of Yehudah, lamentation…."³ "Yehudah went into exile from his homeland"⁴—went into exile. "The king [of Ashur] exiled Yisroel to Ashur" ⁵—exiled others.

footnotes:
¹ Such as גָלָה ,רָבָה ,פָנָה.
² Shemos, 1:20.
³ Eichah, 2:5. Cf. Rashi there.
⁴ Melachim 2, 25:21.
⁵ Melachim 2, 17:6.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Judges 15:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:6:1)


**חֲתַן הַתִּמְנִי.** חֲתַן הַפְּלִשְׁתִּי אֲשֶׁר בְּתִמְנָה (לעיל יד א):


**The son-in-law of the Timnite.** The son-in-law of the Pelishtite from Timnah.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Judges 15:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:7:1)


**אִם תַּעֲשׂוּן כָּזֹאת.** וְשֶׁמָּא רְגִילִין אַתֶּם בְּכָךְ, לָתֵת אִשְׁתּוֹ שֶׁל זֶה לָזֶה:


**Is this the way you behave.** Perhaps you are accustomed to this—shifting wives from one to another.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Judges 15:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:8:1)


**שׁוֹק עַל יָרֵךְ.** (תרגום:) פָּרְשִׁין עִם רַגְלָאִין, רוֹכֵב סוּס אֵינוֹ נִשְׁעָן עַל יְרֵכוֹ כִּי אִם עַל שׁוֹקוֹ, רֶגֶל אֶחָד נָתוּן בַּבַּרְזֶל הַתָּלוּי בַּמֶּרְכֶּבֶת:


**Cavalry and infantry [lit. calf and thigh].** "Horsemen and foot soldiers."⁶ The horseman is not supported by the thigh, but by the calf of the foot inserted into the metal stirrup hanging from the saddle.

footnotes:
⁶ This is Targum Yonasan’s translation. “Calf” refers to the horseman, supported by the calf, as Rashi explains, and “thigh” refers to the footsoldier, whose weight is supported by the thigh.


###### Rashi on Judges 15:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:8:2)


**בִּסְעִיף.** בְּנִקְרַת סֶלַע עֵיטָם, וַחֲבֵרוֹ (ישעיהו נז:ה): סְעִיפֵי הַסְּלָעִים, (שם י:לג): מְסָעֵף פֹּארָה:


**On the promontory** of a crevice of the Eitam cliff. Similarly, "The promontories of the cliffs,"⁷ "[He] shall remove its branches."⁸

footnotes:
⁷ Yeshaya, 2:21
⁸ Ibid, 10:33.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Judges 15:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:9:1)


**וַיִּנָּטְשׁוּ.** וַיִּתְפַּזְּרוּ:


**They fanned out.** They dispersed.


###### Rashi on Judges 15:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:9:2)


**בַּלֶּחִי.** שֵׁם מָקוֹם:


**At Lechi.** The name of a place⁹

footnotes:
⁹ Not literally “cheek”.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Judges 15:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:10:1)


**לָמָה עֲלִיתֶם עָלֵינוּ.** הֲלֹא עֲבָדִים אֲנַחְנוּ לָכֶם:


**Why have you ascended against us.** Are we not enslaved by you?


###### Rashi on Judges 15:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:10:2)


**לֶאֱסוֹר אֶת שִׁמְשׁוֹן.** שֶׁתַּאַסְרוּהוּ וְתַסְגִּירוּהוּ לָנוּ:


**To tie down Shimshon.** In order that you tie him down and deliver him to us.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Judges 15:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:13:1)


**לֹא כִּי אָסוֹר נֶאֱסָרְךָ.** לֹא נַהֲרוֹג אוֹתְךָ, כִּי אִם אָסוֹר נֶאֱסָרְךָ:


**No! We shall merely tie you down.** We shall not execute you, but merely tie you down.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Judges 15:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:14:1)


**רוּחַ ה׳.** (תרגום:) רוּחַ גְּבוּרָה מִן קֳדָם ה׳:


**The spirit of Adonoy.** A spirit of power, emanating from Adonoy.¹⁰

footnotes:
¹⁰ This is Targum Yonasan’s translation.


###### Rashi on Judges 15:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:14:2)


**אֲסוּרָיו.** קִשּׁוּרָיו:


**His bonds** which tied him down.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Judges 15:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:15:1)


**טְרִיָּה.** לַחָה, וְרָאִיתִי בְּסֵפֶר רְפוּאוֹת שֶׁקּוֹרֵא לַלֵּחָה שֶׁיּוֹצֵא מִן הַמַּכָּה: ׳טְרִיָּה׳:


**Moist.** Wet. I saw a medical lexicon which refers to pus oozing from a wound as **טְרִיָה**.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Judges 15:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:16:1)


**חֲמוֹר חֲמֹרָתָיִם.** בִּלְחִי הַחֲמוֹר לִצְבּוֹר צְבָרִים רַבִּים, (תרגום:) בְּלוּעָא דִחמָרָא רְמֵיתִנוּן דְּגוֹרִין:


**I amassed mounds** with the jawbone of a donkey, amassing numerous mounds. "With the jawbone of a donkey I hurled them into mounds."¹¹

footnotes:
¹¹ This is Targum Yonasan’s translation.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Judges 15:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:19:1)


**אֶת הַמַּכְתֵּשׁ.** גּוּמָּא שֶׁהַשֵּׁן יוֹשֶׁבֶת בָּהּ, עֲשׂוּיָה כְּעֵין מַכְתֶּשֶׁת:


**The socket [lit. "the mortar"].** The socket where the tooth is set is shaped like a mortar.


###### Rashi on Judges 15:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/15:19:2)


**עֵין הַקּוֹרֵא.** מַעְיָן שֶׁבָּא עַל יְדֵי זַעֲקַת הַקּוֹרֵא אֶל ה׳:


**Ein Hakorei.** "The wellspring which materialized through the cry of the one who called out to Adonoy."



## Chapter 16


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Judges 16:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:2:1)


**לַעַזָּתִים לֵאמֹר.** לְאַנְשֵׁי עַזָּה נֶאֱמַר:


**The Azites were informed.** It was said to the people of Aza.


###### Rashi on Judges 16:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:2:2)


**וַיִּתְחָרְשׁוּ.** נָהֲגוּ עַצְמָן בִּשְׁתִיקָה:


**They were silent [lit. "They were muted"].** They acted in silence.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Judges 16:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:3:1)


**וַיִּסָּעֵם.** וַיַּעַקְרֵם מִמְּקוֹמָם:


**Uprooted them [lit. "moved them"].** He uprooted them from their places.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Judges 16:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:7:1)


**יְתָרִים.** חֲבָלִים דַּקִּים:


**Cords.** Thin strings.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Judges 16:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:9:1)


**וְלֹא נוֹדַע כֹּחוֹ.** לֹא הוּצְרַךְ לְהִתְחַזֵּק בְּנַתְּקוֹ אוֹתָם:


**The source of his strength was not revealed.** It was not necessary for him to exert himself when he snapped them.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Judges 16:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:11:1)


**בַּעֲבוֹתִים.** חֲבָלִים גַּסִּים:


**With ropes.** Thick rope.¹

footnotes:
¹ Unlike the thin string specified in v. 7.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Judges 16:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:13:1)


**מַחְלְפוֹת.** קְוֻצּוֹת, פלוני״ש בְּלַעַ״ז:


**Tresses** Locks. "Pelous" in old French.


###### Rashi on Judges 16:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:13:2)


**עִם הַמַּסֶּכֶת.** עֵץ שֶׁהַגַּרְדִּי מֵיסֵךְ בּוֹ אֶת הַשְּׁתִי, וְשֵׁם הָעֵץ אנשובל״א מַסֶּכֶת, אורטר״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**With the mounting pin.** A wooden rod which the weaver uses to mount the threads of the warp. The name of the wood is "ensouple", of the pin, "ourdissuire".



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Judges 16:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:16:1)


**וַתְּאַלְצֵהוּ.** דְּחָקַתּוֹ, וְאֵין לוֹ דִּמְיוֹן:


**And goaded him.** She oppressed him. There is no similar word.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Judges 16:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:18:1)


**וַתֵּרֶא דְלִילָה כִּי הִגִּיד לָהּ.** נִכָּרִין דִּבְרֵי אֱמֶת:


**Delilah saw that he had confided to her.** One instinctively recognizes the truth.²

footnotes:
² Sotah, 9:b



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Judges 16:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:19:1)


**וַתִּקְרָא לָאִישׁ.** לִשְׁלוּחָן שֶׁל סְרָנִים:


**[She] summoned the man.** The nobles' agent.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Judges 16:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:21:1)


**וַיּוֹרִידוּ אוֹתוֹ עַזָּתָה.** מְקוֹם תְּחִלַּת קִלְקוּלוֹ:


**They brought him down to Aza.** The location where his ruin began³⁴

footnotes:
³ V.l.
⁴ Sotah, 9:b.


###### Rashi on Judges 16:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:21:2)


**וַיְהִי טוֹחֵן.** בָּרֵחַיִם, מְלָאכָה כְּבֵדָה וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ (סוטה י א) פֵּרְשׁוּ מַה שֶּׁפֵּרְשׁוּ:


**He became the miller. With grinding stones—**arduous labor. The Rabbis interpret this after their fashion.⁵

footnotes:
⁵ According to their interpretation, the Pelishtites brought their wives to the prison to be impregnated by Shimshon, (Sotah, 10:a)



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Judges 16:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:25:1)


**בֵּין הָעַמּוּדִים.** אֲשֶׁר הַבַּיִת נָכוֹן עֲלֵיהֶם:


**Between the pillars** which supported the building.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Judges 16:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:26:1)


**וַהֲמִישֵׁנִי.** פאלפא״ר בְּלַעַ״ז, כְּמוֹ (בראשית כז:יב): ״אוּלַי יְמֻשֵּׁנִי״:


**And give me a hold.** "Palpare" in old French, as in, "Perhaps my father will touch me."⁶

footnotes:
⁶ Bereishis, 27:12.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Judges 16:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:28:1)


**זָכְרֵנִי נָא.** זְכֹר עֶשְׂרִים שָׁנָה שֶׁשָּׁפַטְתִּי אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל, וְלֹא אָמַרְתִּי לְאֶחָד מֵהֶם הַעֲבֵר לִי מַקֵּל זֶה מִמָּקוֹם לְמָקוֹם:


**Please remember me.** Remember the twenty years that I judged Yisroel without asking any of them to carry a staff from place to place.⁷

footnotes:
⁷ Sotah, 10:a.


###### Rashi on Judges 16:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:28:2)


**נְקַם אַחַת מִשְּׁתֵי עֵינַי.** וּשְׂכַר עַיִן הַשֵּׁנִית הַנַּח לִי לָעוֹלָם הַבָּא, וְכָאן פְּרַע לִי שְׂכַר אַחַת מֵהֶן:


**Avenge one of my two eyes.** The reward for my second eye reserve for me in the World to Come. At present, grant me the reward for one of them.⁸

footnotes:
⁸ Yalkut, 71.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Judges 16:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/16:29:1)


**וַיִּלְפֹּת.** וַיֹּאחַז, אינברצ״א בְּלַעַ״ז, כְּמוֹ (רות ג:ח): ״וַיֶּחֱרַד הָאִישׁ וַיִּלָּפֵת״, נֶאֱחַז בִּזְרוֹעוֹת הָאִשָּׁה:


**Shimshon embraced.** He grasped, "embrasser" in old French, as in "The man trembled, and was embraced"⁹—he was embraced in a woman's arms.

footnotes:
⁹ Rus, 3:8.



## Chapter 17


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Judges 17:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:1:1)


**וַיְהִי אִישׁ מֵהַר אֶפְרָיִם.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנִּכְתְּבוּ שְׁתֵּי פָּרָשִׁיּוֹת הַלָּלוּ בְּסוֹף הַסֵּפֶר, שֶׁל מִיכָה וְשֶׁל פִּלֶגֶשׁ בַּגִּבְעָה, בִּתְחִלַּת הַשּׁוֹפְטִים הָיָה, בִּימֵי עָתְנִיאֵל בֶּן קְנַז, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (שופטים יח:לא): ״וַיָּשִׂימוּ לָהֶם אֶת פֶּסֶל מִיכָה וְגוֹ׳ כָּל יְמֵי הֱיוֹת בֵּית אֱלֹהִים בְּשִׁילֹה״, לָמַדְנוּ שֶׁכָּל יְמֵי שִׁילֹה הָיָה דְּמוּת שֶׁל מִיכָה, וּבְפִלֶגֶשׁ בַּגִּבְעָה נֶאֱמַר עַל יְבוּס שֶׁבִּירוּשָׁלַיִם (שופטים יט:יב): ״לֹא נָסוּר אֶל עִיר נָכְרִי״, לָמַדְנוּ שֶׁעֲדַיִן לֹא כָּבְשׁוּ אֶת יְרוּשָׁלַיִם:


**There was a man from Mount Ephraim.** Although both incidents are recorded at the conclusion of this book, the episodes of Michah and the concubine at Givoh took place at the beginning of the period of Judges, during the days of Asniel ben Kenaz,¹ as it is said, "They appointed, for themselves, the figure of Michah, etc., during the entire period of the *Mishkon* at Shiloh."² We learn that, throughout the period of Shiloh, the graven figure of Michah was in existence.³ Concerning the concubine at Givoh, it is said, about Yevus of Jerusalem, "Let us not turn to an alien city"⁴ we learn that Jerusalem had not yet been conquered.⁵⁶

footnotes:
¹ Asniel was the first of the judges.
³ Thus encompassing the period of Asniel, as his tenure began after the death of Yehoshua, when the Mishkon had already been established at Shiloh.
⁵ It was later conquered by the tribe of Judah (1:8), during the time of Asniel (there, v.13).
⁶ Seder Olam, 12.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Judges 17:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:2:1)


**אֲשֶׁר לֻקַּח לָךְ.** אֲשֶׁר נִגְנַב מִמֵּךְ, וְיֵשׁ לוֹ דוֹמֶה (שמואל א כא:ו): ״כִּי אִשָּׁה עֲצוּרָה לָנוּ״, עֲצוּרָה מִמֶּנּוּ:


**Stolen from you [lit. "to you"].** Stolen from you.⁷ Similarly,⁸ "For women were withheld 'to' us,"—'from' us.

footnotes:
⁷ לך, lit. “to you”, may also be interpreted as “from you”.
⁸ Shmuel 1, 21:6


###### Rashi on Judges 17:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:2:2)


**וְאַתְּ אָלִית.** קִלְלַת כָּל מִי שֶׁגְּנָבוֹ, וְגַם אָמַרְתְּ הַקְּלָלָה בְּאָזְנַי:


**When you cursed.** You cursed whoever stole it, and even uttered the curse in my ear.


###### Rashi on Judges 17:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:2:3)


**הִנֵּה הַכֶּסֶף.** אֲנִי מוֹדֶה לָךְ עָלָיו שֶׁהוּא בְּיָדִי שֶׁאֲנִי לְקַחְתִּיו:


**Look, the silver.** I confess to you, concerning it, that it is in my possession, as I stole it.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Judges 17:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:3:1)


**וַיָּשֶׁב אֶת אֶלֶף וּמֵאָה הַכֶּסֶף לְאִמּוֹ.** אָמַר לְהַחֲזִירוֹ לָהּ, וּבַמָּקוֹם שֶׁהוּא שָׁם הֲרֵי הוּא בִּרְשׁוּתֵךְ:


**He returned the eleven hundred silver pieces to his mother.** He verbalized his intent to return it to her, hence, wherever it was, it reverted to her possession.


###### Rashi on Judges 17:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:3:2)


**הַקְדֵּשׁ הִקְדַּשְׁתִּי אֶת הַכֶּסֶף מִיָּדִי לִבְנִי.** קִבַּלְתִּי עָלַי לְמָסְרוֹ מִיָּדִי לְיָדְךָ לְשֵׁם דְּמוּת וּמַסֵּכָה, תְּנֵהוּ לִי עַתָּה וּמִיָּדִי אֲנִי אֲשִׁיבֶנּוּ אֵלֶיךָ יֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים שֶׁהָאִשָּׁה הַזֹּאת הִיא דְּלִילָה, לְפִי שֶׁכָּתוּב (לעיל טז:ה): ״אֶלֶף וּמֵאָה כֶּסֶף״, וְטָעוּת הוּא בְּיָדָם, שֶׁהַרְבֵּה שָׁנִים קָדַם מִיכָה לְשִׁמְשׁוֹן, אַךְ הַפָּרָשִׁיּוֹת נִסְמְכוּ עַל הַכֶּסֶף הָרָע שֶׁשָּׁוֶה כָּאן וְכָאן, וְכֶסֶף שֶׁל פּוּרְעָנוּת הָיוּ שְׁנֵיהֶם:


**I expressly consecrated the silver, from my hand to my son's.** I resolved to deliver it from my hand to yours for the purpose of making an image and a molten figure. Give it to me now, so that I may return it from my hand to you. Some say that this woman was Delilah, since Scripture specifies eleven hundred pieces of silver⁹. However, they are in error, as Michah preceded Shimshon by many years.¹⁰ But the episodes are juxtaposed because of the corrupt silver, which was of equal value in each of the incidents. The silver inflicted injury in both cases.

footnotes:
⁹ See above 16:5.
¹⁰ See Rashi, v.l.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Judges 17:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:5:1)


**בֵּית אֱלֹהִים.** בֵּית עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה, וְכֵן כָּל ׳אֱלֹהִים׳ הָאֲמוּרִים בְּפָרָשָׁה זוֹ חוֹל, חוּץ מִזֶּה (שופטים יח:לא): ״כָּל יְמֵי הֱיוֹת בֵּית הָאֱלֹהִים בְּשִׁילֹה״ (שבועות לה ב):


**An idolatrous temple** [lit. "temple of Elohim"]. A temple of idol worship. Similarly, "god" throughout this chapter is profane, except for the following: "Throughout the time that the Sanctuary of God was at Shiloh."¹¹¹²

footnotes:
¹² Shevuos, 35:b.


###### Rashi on Judges 17:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:5:2)


**וַיְמַלֵּא אֶת יַד אַחַד.** חֲנָכוֹ לַעֲבוֹדַת הַדְּמוּת לִהְיוֹת כֹּהֵן לְפָנָיו, כָּל חִנּוּךְ דָּבָר שֶׁאָדָם נִכְנָס בּוֹ לְשֵׁם גְּדֻלָּה קָרוּי מִלּוּי יָדַיִם, רישוושטי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז; וּכְשֶׁנּוֹתְנִין פְּקִידוּת לְאָדָם, קוֹרִין לְמַתָּן רישוישטי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**And inducted [lit. "filled the hands of"] one.** He inducted him into the service of the image, to serve as its priest. Any induction process which a person enters for the purpose of achieving a position of prominence is described as "filling the hands,"¹³ "revetir" in old French. When a person is charged with a position, it is described as "reveture" in old French.

footnotes:
¹³ See Shemos, 28:41.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Judges 17:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:7:1)


**מִמִּשְׁפַּחַת יְהוּדָה.** וְהוּא לֵוִי מִן הָאֵם וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ אָמְרוּ (בבא בתרא קט ב): לְפִי שֶׁעָשָׂה מַעֲשֵׂה מְנַשֶּׁה שֶׁבָּא מִיהוּדָה, קְרָאוֹ מִמִּשְׁפַּחַת יְהוּדָה, וְהוּא לֵוִי בֶּן גֵּרְשׁוֹם בֶּן מֹשֶׁה רַבֵּינוּ הָיָה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ בָּעִנְיָן (שופטים יח:ל): ״וִיהוֹנָתָן בֶּן גֵּרְשׁוֹם וְגוֹ׳״:


**Of the clan of Yehudah.** He was a Leivi maternally.¹⁴ However, the Rabbis¹⁵ interpret that, because he behaved like Menasheh,¹⁶ who was descended from Yehudah, he is described as "of the clan of Yehudah." He was actually a Leivi, the son of Gershom, son of our master Moshe, as stated explicitly below, "Yehonasan, son of Gershom, etc."¹⁷

footnotes:
¹⁴ Paternally, he was descended from Yehudah. This explains why he was both “of the clan of Yehudah”, and a Levi.
¹⁵ Bava Basra, 109:b.
¹⁶ The king who instituted idol worship throughout Eretz Yisroel. (Melachim 2, 21).



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Judges 17:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:8:1)


**לַעֲשׂוֹת דַּרְכּוֹ.** דֶּרֶךְ הֲלִיכָתוֹ:


**To make his way.** The way by which he would journey.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Judges 17:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:9:1)


**בַּאֲשֶׁר אֶמְצָא.** פַּרְנָסָתִי לְהִשְׂתַּכֵּר:


**Anywhere I will find** a profitable livelihood.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Judges 17:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:10:1)


**לַיָּמִים.** לְסוֹף שָׁנָה:


**Per year** At the year's end.


###### Rashi on Judges 17:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:10:2)


**וְעֵרֶךְ בְּגָדִים.** זוּג חֲלִיפַת בְּגָדִים שֶׁהֵם עֵרֶךְ וְסֵדֶר לְכָל בְּנֵי אָדָם כְּפִי הָרָאוּי לָהֶם לְשָׁנָה:


**An appropriate wardrobe** A pair of outfits appropriate for everyone's yearly requirements.


###### Rashi on Judges 17:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:10:3)


**וְעֵרֶךְ בְּגָדִים.** אפרליימנ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז:


**An appropriate wardrobe.** "Appareillement" in old French.


###### Rashi on Judges 17:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:10:4)


**וּמִחְיָתֶךָ.** וּמְזוֹנוֹתֶיךָ:


**And your board** Your meals.


###### Rashi on Judges 17:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/17:10:5)


**וַיֵּלֶךְ הַלֵּוִי.** אַחַר עֲצָתוֹ:


**The Levi acquiesced [lit. went]** Followed his advice.



## Chapter 18


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Judges 18:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:1:1)


**כִּי לֹא נָפְלָה לוֹ.** יְרֻשָּׁה כְּפִי הָרָאוּי לָהֶם בְּאֶרֶץ מְכֻבֶּשֶׁת, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בִּיהוֹשֻׁעַ (יהושע יט:מז): ״וַיֵּצֵא גְבוּל בְּנֵי דָן מֵהֶם״, וְאַף מִכָּאן יֵשׁ לִלְמֹד שֶׁהָיָה מַעֲשֶׂה זֶה בִּתְחִלַּת הַשּׁוֹפְטִים מִיָּד:


**As there had not fallen to their lot.** A suitable inheritance for them in the conquered territory, as it is said in Yehoshua, "The boundry of Bnei Don extended from them."¹² This, too, teaches us that this episode took place at the very beginning of the period of the judges.³

footnotes:
¹ Yehoshua, 19:47. See Rashi there.
² When the apportioning of the land among the tribes was completed.
³ In addition to the evidence presented by Rashi above, 17:1.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Judges 18:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:3:1)


**מִי הֱבִיאֲךָ הֲלֹם.** יֵשׁ כָּאן תְּמִיהוֹת הַרְבֵּה, (בבא בתרא קי:) לֹא מִזַּרְעוֹ שֶׁל מֹשֶׁה רַבֵּינוּ אַתָּה, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בּוֹ (שמות ג:ה): ״אַל תִּקְרַב הֲלֹם״, (שם ד:יא): ״מִי שָׂם פֶּה לָאָדָם״, (שם ד:ב): ״מַה זֶּה בְיָדֶךָ״:


**Who brought you here.** There are numerous questions here. Are you not descended from our teacher Moshe, to whom it was said, "Do not come close here,"⁴ "Who provided mankind with the mouth?"⁵ and "What is this in your hand?"⁶

footnotes:
⁴ Shemos, 3:5.הֲלוֹם, “here”, in our passage corresponds with הֲלוֹם, “here”, in the statement to Moshe.
⁵ Ibid, 4:11. פֶז in our passage coresponds with פֶה “mouth”, there.
⁶ Ibid, 4:2. מָה…בָּזֶה corresponds with מַה זֶה there.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Judges 18:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:5:1)


**שְׁאַל נָא בֵאלֹהִים.** בַּתְּרָפִים שֶׁמְּדַבְּרִים עַל יְדֵי מְכַשְּׁפוֹת:


**Please inquire of God** through the figurines, which communicate by sorcery.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Judges 18:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:6:1)


**נֹכַח ה׳ דַּרְכְּכֶם.** נֹכַח הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא גָּלוּי, כִּי זֶה אֵין בּוֹ מַמָּשׁ:


**The route you will follow is before Adonoy** It is revealed before the Holy One, blessed is He, but these [figurines] are worthless.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Judges 18:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:7:1)


**וְאֵין מַכְלִים דָּבָר.** אֵין אֶחָד מֵהֶם צָרִיךְ לַחֲבֵרוֹ שֶׁיַּחֲזִירֶנּוּ רֵיקָם וְיַכְלִימֶנּוּ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בְּסוֹף הָעִנְיָן (פסוק י): ״אֵין מַחְסוֹר כָּל דָּבָר״, כָּךְ שָׁמַעְתִּי:


**With nothing to put them to shame.** No one ever needed anothers' help, who might have rebuffed him empty handedly, thus humiliating him, as stated below, "Nothing is lacking."⁷ So I have heard.

footnotes:
⁷ Below v.10.


###### Rashi on Judges 18:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:7:2)


**יוֹרֵשׁ עֶצֶר.** יוֹרְשִׁים מוּעָטִים, שֶׁאִם יַהַרְגוּ אֶת אֵלּוּ, אֵין עוֹד עוֹרְרִין נִלְחָמִים עִם הַמַּחֲזִיקִים בַּנַּחֲלָה:


**There is a dearth of heirs.** There are few heirs, so that if they should be killed, no other claimants will wage war against those who occupy the territory.


###### Rashi on Judges 18:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:7:3)


**וּרְחוֹקִים הֵמָּה.** מֵעֶזְרַת צִידוֹנִים:


**They were distant.** From the support of the Tzidonians.


###### Rashi on Judges 18:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:7:4)


**וְדָבָר אֵין לָהֶם עִם אָדָם.** שׁוּם בְּרִית כְּרוּתָה שֶׁיָּבֹאוּ לְעֶזְרָתָם לְעֵת הַצֹּרֶךְ:


**And they were not allied with anyone.** They had not entered into any treaty requiring others to come to their assistance in times of need.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Judges 18:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:8:1)


**מָה אַתֶּם.** כְּלוֹמַר מָה אַתֶּם מְשִׁיבִים דָּבָר, מַה מְּצָאתֶם בַּמָּקוֹם שֶׁנָּפַל לָנוּ גוֹרָלֵנוּ, מָה הָעָם, הֶחָזָק הוּא הֲרָפֶה:


**What is your response?** As if to say, "what is your response?" What have you found, in the territory apportioned to us? Are the people strong or weak?'



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Judges 18:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:9:1)


**וְאַתֶּם מַחְשִׁים.** מִתְעַצְּלִים:


**You are apathetic** Lazy.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Judges 18:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:21:1)


**וְאֶת הַכְּבוּדָּה.** מַשָּׂאוֹת, כָּל דָּבָר שֶׁהוּא כָּבֵד בַּהֲלִיכָתוֹ שֶׁאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לָרוּץ אִם יִרְדְּפוּ בְּנֵי הַמָּקוֹם אַחֲרֵיהֶם:


**The bulky cargo.** The loads. Anything which moved ponderously, thus making it impossible to move quickly should the local inhabitants pursue them.


###### Rashi on Judges 18:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:21:2)


**הַכְּבוּדָּה.** פיזנטו״מא בְּלַעַ״ז:


**The bulky cargo.** "Pezantume" in old French.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Judges 18:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:23:1)


**וַיַּסֵּבּוּ פְּנֵיהֶם.** בְּנֵי דָן אֶל הַקּוֹרְאִים אֲלֵיהֶם:


**They turned their faces.** The sons of Don turned towards those who shouted at them.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Judges 18:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:24:1)


**וּמַה לִּי עוֹד.** וּמַה נִּשְׁאַר לִי עוֹד:


**What else do I have.** What else remains for me?



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Judges 18:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:25:1)


**אֲנָשִׁים מָרֵי נֶפֶשׁ.** כַּעֲסָנִים שֶׁיֵּשׁ בְּסִיעָתֵנוּ:


**Bitter tempered men.** There are angry men in our company.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Judges 18:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:27:1)


**לַיִשׁ.** שֵׁם הָעִיר, וּבְסֵפֶר יְהוֹשֻׁעַ (יהושע יט:מז) קוֹרֵא שְׁמָהּ ׳לֶשֶׁם׳, עַל שֵׁם שֶׁמָּצְאוּ שָׁם אֶבֶן טוֹבָה שֶׁשְּׁמָהּ ׳לֶשֶׁם׳, וְהִיא הָיְתָה עַל הַחֹשֶׁן לְשֵׁבֶט דָּן, שְׁמוֹ כָּתוּב עַל ׳לֶשֶׁם׳, וְיָדְעוּ שֶׁבֶּאֱמֶת הִיא נַחֲלָתָם:


**Laish.** The city's name. The book of Yehoshua entitles it "Leshem,"⁸ because they discovered, there, a precious gem called "leshem." This was inlaid in the Choshen plate,⁹ and symbolized the tribe of Don, whose name was inscribed on it. Thus, they were certain that this was actually their ancestral property.

footnotes:
⁸ Yehoshua 19:47.
⁹ The sacred Urim Vetumim oracles, which spelled out the Divine message for those inquirii of Adonoy. (Yoma, 73:b).



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Judges 18:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:30:1)


**בֶּן מְנַשֶּׁה.** מִפְּנֵי כְּבוֹדוֹ שֶׁל מֹשֶׁה כָּתוּב נוּ״ן לְשַׁנּוֹת אֶת הַשֵּׁם, וְנִכְתַּב תְּלוּיָה לוֹמַר שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה מְנַשֶּׁה אֶלָּא מֹשֶׁה:


**Ben Menasheh.** In deference to Moshe, the letter "nun" was included, thus altering the name. It is scripturally suspended to indicate that he was actually not Menasheh, but Moshe.¹⁰

footnotes:
¹⁰ Bava Basra 109b.


###### Rashi on Judges 18:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/18:30:2)


**עַד יוֹם גְּלוֹת הָאָרֶץ.** בִּימֵי סַנְחֵרִיב בְּגָלוּת רִאשׁוֹנָה:


**Until the day of the exile from the land.** During the time of Sancheriv, at the first exile.



## Chapter 19


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Judges 19:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/19:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/19:2:1)


**וַתִּזְנֶה עָלָיו פִּילַגְשׁוֹ.** זָנְתָה מִבֵּיתוֹ אֶל הַחוּץ כָּל לְשׁוֹן זְנוּת אֵינוֹ אֶלָּא לְשׁוֹן ׳יוֹצֵאת׳ (תרגום אונקלוס בראשית לד:לא): נַפְקַת בָּרָא, יוֹצֵאת מִבַּעְלָהּ לֶאֱהוֹב אֶת אֲחֵרִים:


**His concubine strayed away from him** She strayed away from his home to the outside. The word **זְנוּת**, "promiscuity," always denotes departure—"One who departs to the outside,"¹ who leaves her husband and loves another.

footnotes:
¹ This is Targum Yonasan’s rendition of זוֹנָה, “promiscuous woman.”



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Judges 19:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/19:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/19:6:1)


**הוֹאֶל נָא וְלִין.** הַלַּיְלָה:


**Give your assent, and remain overnight.** Tonight.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Judges 19:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/19:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/19:9:1)


**רָפָה.** הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ מִגְּבוּרָתוֹ וְנָטָה לַעֲרוֹב:


**Has waned.** The sun [has waned] from full strength, and has begun setting.


###### Rashi on Judges 19:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/19:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/19:9:2)


**הִנֵּה חֲנוֹת הַיּוֹם.** עֵת שֶׁכָּל הוֹלְכֵי דְרָכִים נוֹטִים לַחֲנוֹת בַּבַּיִת:


**It is the time of day for encamping.** The time when all travellers turn in to encamp indoors.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Judges 19:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/19:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/19:12:1)


**עַד גִּבְעָה.** גִּבְעַת בִּנְיָמִן:


**To Giv'oh.** Giv'oh of Binyomin.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Judges 19:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/19:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/19:13:1)


**וְלַנּוּ.** כְּמוֹ וְלַנְנוּ, דַּגְשׁוּת הַנּוּ״ן בִּמְקוֹם נוּ״ן שְׁנִיָּה:


**We will spend the night.** As in **וְלַנְנוּ**. The period punctuating the **נ** serves as a replacement for a second **נ**.²

footnotes:
² The suffix נו denotes the plural usage. When the letter נ appears in the root, as in לין, the additional נ is dropped, and is replaced by a period in the original נ.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Judges 19:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/19:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/19:18:1)


**וְאֶת בֵּית ה׳.** לְשִׁילֹה אֲנִי הוֹלֵךְ:


**To the Sanctuary of Adonoy.** I am going to Shiloh.³

footnotes:
³ The Mishkon was at Shiloh. (Yehosnua, 18:1).



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Judges 19:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/19:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/19:21:1)


**וַיָּבָל לַחֲמוֹרִים.** וַיִּתֵּן יְבוּל לַחֲמוֹרִים, ׳וַיָּבָל׳ אפרוונד״ר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**And fed produce to the donkeys.** He gave produce to the donkeys. He fed produce, "aprovender" in old French.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Judges 19:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/19:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/19:22:1)


**וְנֵדָעֶנּוּ.** מִשְׁכַּב זָכוּר:


**We want to have relations with him** to sodomize him.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Judges 19:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/19:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/19:28:1)


**וְאֵין עוֹנֶה.** כִּי מֵתָה:


**There was no answer,** for she was dead.



## Chapter 20


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Judges 20:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:2:1)


**פִּנּוֹת כָּל הָעָם.** (תרגום:) רֵישֵׁי כָּל עַמָּא:


**The leaders of the entire people.** The heads of the entire people.¹

footnotes:
¹ This is Targum Yonasan’s translation.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Judges 20:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:5:1)


**דִּמּוּ לַהֲרֹג.** כִּוְּנוּ לַהֲרֹג, אנישמינט״ו בְּלַעַ״ז:


**They thought they would murder me.** They intended to murder me. "Apesment" in old French.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Judges 20:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:10:1)


**לַעֲשׂוֹת לְבוֹאָם.** לְבִיאָתָם זוֹ לְגִבְעַת בִּנְיָמִין ׳כְּכָל הַנְּבָלָה׳ וְגוֹ׳:


**When taking action in their engagement** in this engagement with Givas Binyomin, as befits their entirely loathsome, etc.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Judges 20:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:11:1)


**חֲבֵרִים.** שָׁוִים בְּעֵצָה אַחַת:


**Comrades.** In unified counsel.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Judges 20:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:12:1)


**בְּכָל שִׁבְטֵי בִנְיָמִן.** עֲשָׂרָה שְׁבָטִים נֶחְשְׁבוּ עֲשָׂרָה מִשְׁפְּחוֹתָיו, שֶׁיִּהְיוּ מֵרָחֵל שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר שְׁבָטִים:


**To all the tribes of Binyomin.** Their ten clans were considered ten families, so that twelve tribes would emanate from Rochel.²

footnotes:
² The ten clans and the tribes of Menashe and Efraim.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Judges 20:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:16:1)


**אִטֵּר יַד יְמִינוֹ.** גְּמוּדֵי יַד יְמִינָם, לֹא הָיוּ שׁוֹלְטִים בָּהּ כְּאִלּוּ הָיְתָה אֲטוּמָה, כְּמוֹ (תהלים סט:טז): ״אַל תֶּאְטַר עָלַי בְּאֵר פִּיהָ״:


**With shriveled right arms.** Their right arms were shrunken. They could not utilize them, as if they were closed, as in "Let not the well close its mouth over me."³

footnotes:
³ Tehilim, 69:16.


###### Rashi on Judges 20:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:16:2)


**כָּל זֶה.** שְׁבַע מֵאוֹת אִישׁ הַלָּלוּ:


**Each of them** These seven hundred men.


###### Rashi on Judges 20:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:16:3)


**אֶל הַשַּׂעֲרָה.** לְחוּט הַשֵּׂעָר:


**At a hair.** A strand of hair.


###### Rashi on Judges 20:16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:16:4)


**וְלֹא יַחֲטִא.** לֹא יִשְׁגֶּה:


**And not miss.** And not err.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Judges 20:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:18:1)


**יְהוּדָה בַתְּחִלָּה.** אֲבָל לֹא בָחֲנוּ לִשְׁאוֹל אִם לְנַצֵּחַ אִם לִינָּצֵחַ, וּבָאַחֲרוֹנָה שֶׁבָּחֲנוּ, אָמַר (פסוק כח): ״עֲלוּ כִּי מָחָר אֶתְּנֶנּוּ בְיָדֶךָ״, הִשְׁכִּימוּ וְהִשְׁלִימוּ דִּבְרֵיהֶם:


**Yehudah first.** But they did not try to inquire whether they would be victorious or vanquished. When they ultimately tried, He replied, "Go up, for tomorrow I shall deliver them into your hand." They were consistent, and fulfilled their promise.⁴

footnotes:
⁴ V.28.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Judges 20:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:21:1)


**וַיַּשְׁחִיתוּ.** נֶעֶנְשׁוּ עַל שֶׁלֹּא קִנְּאוּ כְּמוֹ כֵן בְּמַעֲשֵׂה דְּמוּת מִיכָה שֶׁהָיָה כְּבָר:


**They crushed.** They were punished because they were not equally vengeful over the incident of the figure of Michah,⁵ which had already taken place.⁶

footnotes:
⁵ Chapters 17, 18.
⁶ Sanhedrin, 103:b.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on Judges 20:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:33:1)


**בְּבַעַל תָּמָר.** בְּמֵישַׁר יְרִיחוֹ:


**Baal Tamar.** The plain of Yericho.⁷

footnotes:
⁷ “Tamar”, lit. “date”, alludes to Yericho, “The city of dates.” Devarim, 34:3.


###### Rashi on Judges 20:33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:33:2)


**מֵגִיחַ.** נִמְשָׁךְ, כְּמוֹ (איוב מ:כג): ״יָגִיחַ יַרְדֵּן אֶל פִּיהוּ״; (תהלים כב:י): ״גֹחִי מִבָּטֶן״:


**Poured.** Extended themselves, as in "He shall draw the Yardein into his mouth,"⁸ "You drew me from the womb."⁹

footnotes:
⁸ Eyov, 40:23.
⁹ Tehilim, 22:10.


###### Rashi on Judges 20:33:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:33:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:33:3)


**מִמַּעֲרֵה גֶבַע.** מִמָּקוֹם צַר, עֶרְוָתָהּ וְגִלּוּיָהּ שֶׁל גִּבְעַת בִּנְיָמִין, מְקוֹם תּוֹרְפָה שֶׁלָּהּ שֶׁהִיא נוֹחָה לִיכָּבֵשׁ מִשָּׁם, כְּמוֹ (בראשית מב:ט): ״לִרְאוֹת אֶת עֶרְוַת הָאָרֶץ בָּאתֶם״:


**Maareh Gev'ah.** From a narrow pass, the "nakedness,"¹⁰ nakedness. or exposed overlook of Givas Binyomin; its uncovered part from which it was defenseless against conquest, as in, "You have come to gaze at the nakedness of the land."¹¹

footnotes:
¹⁰ “Maareh” is from “Ervah,”
¹¹ Beraishis, 42:9. Yoseif accused his brothers of spying, claiming. that they were seeking Egypt’s “nakedness,” or exposed, defenseless areas.



### Verse 36

###### Rashi on Judges 20:36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:36:1)


**וַיִּתְּנוּ אִישׁ יִשְׂרָאֵל.** אֲשֶׁר בַּשָּׂדֶה, מָקוֹם לְבִנְיָמִן לִבְרֹחַ אֶל הַגִּבְעָה, כִּי סָמְכוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל עַל הָאוֹרֵב שֶׁיֵּצְאוּ לִקְרָאתָם:


**The Yisroelite troops gave.** Those in the field gave ground to Binyomin to escape to Givoh, since the Yisroelites depended on the ambushers to advance toward them.



### Verse 37

###### Rashi on Judges 20:37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:37:1)


**וַיִּמְשֹׁךְ הָאוֹרֵב.** מָשַׁךְ בַּשּׁוֹפָר; דָּבָר אַחֵר: מָשַׁךְ בַּשּׁוֹפָר הָעָם אֲשֶׁר אִתּוֹ, אֶל הָעִיר:


**The ambushers sounded the signal.** The shofar blast. Another interpretation: With the shofar, they drew the people with them to the city.



### Verse 38

###### Rashi on Judges 20:38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:38:1)


**וְהַמּוֹעֵד הָיָה.** מִתְּחִלָּה הָיָה אוֹת בֵּין יִשְׂרָאֵל וּבֵין הָאוֹרֵב, לִהְיוֹת הָאוֹרֵב מַרְבֶּה לְהַעֲלוֹת עֲשַׁן הָעִיר, וְיָבִינוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל אֲשֶׁר לָכַד הָאוֹרֵב אֶת הַגִּבְעָה:


**A time frame was pre-arranged** At the outset, a signal was arranged between the Yisroelites and the ambushers, whereby the ambushers would raise continuous smoke from the city. The Yisroelites would thus understand that the ambushers had conquered Givoh.


###### Rashi on Judges 20:38:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:38:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:38:2)


**מַשְׂאַת הֶעָשָׁן.** גֹּבַהּ הֶעָשָׁן:


**Clouds of smoke.** High hanging smoke.



### Verse 39

###### Rashi on Judges 20:39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:39:1)


**וַיַּהֲפֹךְ אִישׁ יִשְׂרָאֵל.** עַכְשָׁיו חוֹזֵר לִתְחִלַּת הַדְּבָרִים וְשׁוֹנֶה וּמְפָרֵשׁ, עֶשְׂרִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה אֶלֶף שֶׁכָּתַבְנוּ לְמַעְלָה (פסוק לה), כֵּיצַד נָפְלוּ וְהֵיכָן נָפְלוּ:


**The Yisroelite troops retreated.** Scripture now returns to the beginning of the narrative, reviewing and explaining how the twenty five thousand mentioned above¹² fell, and where they fell.

footnotes:
¹² V.35.



### Verse 42

###### Rashi on Judges 20:42:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:42:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:42:1)


**וַאֲשֶׁר מֵהֶעָרִים.** הָאוֹרֵב שֶׁנִּכְנַס בָּעִיר, מַשְׁחִיתִים אוֹתוֹ בְּתוֹךְ הַשֵּׁבֶט, בְּתוֹךְ הָעִיר:


**While those in the city.** The ambushers who had penetrated the city demolished the tribe from within the city.



### Verse 43

###### Rashi on Judges 20:43:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:43:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:43:1)


**כִּתְּרוּ אֶת בִּנְיָמִן.** הִקִּיפוּ אֶת בִּנְיָמִן כְּכֶתֶר הַמַּקִּיף אֶת הָרֹאשׁ, וְכֵן (תהלים כב:יג): ״אַבִּירֵי בָשָׁן כִּתְּרוּנִי״, וְכֵן (חבקוק א:ד): ״כִּי רָשָׁע מַכְתִּיר אֶת הַצַּדִּיק״, וְכֵן גַּם בִּלְשׁוֹן עֲטָרָה נִקְרֵאת הַקָּפָה (שמואל א כג:כו): ״וְשָׁאוּל וַאֲנָשָׁיו עֹטְרִים אֶל דָּוִד וְאֶל אֲנָשָׁיו לְתָפְשָׂם״:


**They [lit. crowned] Binyomin.** They encircled Binyomin the way a crown encircles the head. Similarly, "The mighty of Bashan encircled me,"¹³ and, "For the villain encircles the saint."¹⁴ Also, the word "wreath" connotes encirclement, as in, "Shaul and his troops encircled [lit. wreathed] David and his troops in order to seize them"¹⁵

footnotes:
¹³ Tehilim, 22:13.
¹⁴ Chabakuk, 1:4.
¹⁵ Shmuel 1, 23:26.


###### Rashi on Judges 20:43:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:43:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:43:2)


**הִרְדִיפֻהוּ.** קוֹרְאִים אַחֲרָיו רְדִיפָה, צוֹעֲקִים זֶה לָזֶה לִרְדֹּף:


**Called for their pursuit.** They called for pursuit behind them—they shouted to each other to pursue.


###### Rashi on Judges 20:43:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:43:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:43:3)


**מְנוּחָה הִדְרִיכֻהוּ.** אֵצֶל הֶעָרִים שֶׁהֵם נוֹחִים בָּהֶם, שָׁם הִדְבִּיקוּהוּ:


**And intercepted them at rest.** At the cities, where they generally rested, they intercepted them.


###### Rashi on Judges 20:43:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:43:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:43:4)


**הִדְרִיכֻהוּ.** הִשִּׂיגוּהוּ, וְיֵשׁ דֻּגְמָתוֹ בִּלְשׁוֹן גְּמָרָא (כתובות ס ב): רְהַט בַּתְרֵיהּ פַּרְסָא בְּחָלָא וְלֹא אַדְרְכֵיהּ:


**Intercepted them.** Caught them. There is a similar term in the Talmud, "He pursued him a parsa in the sand, but did not catch him."¹⁶

footnotes:
¹⁶ Kesubos, 60:b.


###### Rashi on Judges 20:43:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:43:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:43:5)


**עַד נֹכַח הַגִּבְעָה.** מִמִּזְרָחָהּ שֶׁל גִּבְעָה:


**Up to the area facing Givoh.** East of Givoh.



### Verse 44

###### Rashi on Judges 20:44:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:44:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:44:1)


**וַיִּפְּלוּ מִבִּנְיָמִן שְׁמוֹנָה עָשָׂר אֶלֶף.** בַּשָּׂדֶה בְּתוֹךְ הַמִּלְחָמָה:


**There fell, from Binyomin,** eighteen thousand troops In the field, in the thick of battle.



### Verse 45

###### Rashi on Judges 20:45:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:45:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:45:1)


**וַיְעֹלְלֻהוּ.** הֲרִיגָה אַחַר הֲרִיגָה, כְּעוֹלְלוֹת שֶׁאַחַר הַבָּצִיר:


**They gleaned them.** Killing constantly, like plucking gleanings after reaping.


###### Rashi on Judges 20:45:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:45:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:45:2)


**חֲמֵשֶׁת אֲלָפִים אִישׁ.** הֲרֵי עֶשְׂרִים וּשְׁלֹשָׁה אֶלֶף:


**Five thousand troops.** Thus totaling twenty three thousand.


###### Rashi on Judges 20:45:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:45:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:45:3)


**עַד גִּדְעֹם.** שֵׁם מָקוֹם:


**Until Gid'om.** The name of a place.


###### Rashi on Judges 20:45:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:45:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:45:4)


**וַיַּכּוּ מִמֶּנּוּ אַלְפַּיִם אִישׁ.** הֲרֵי עֶשְׂרִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה אֶלֶף הָאָמוּר לְמַעְלָה (פסוק לה), וְהַמֵּאָה הַכְּתוּבִים לְמַעְלָה (שם), לֹא פֵּרַשׁ הֵיכָן נָפְלוּ:


**Striking down, of them, two thousand troops.** Thus totaling twenty-five thousand mentioned above.¹⁷ The remaining one hundred recorded above¹⁸ fell at unnamed locations.

footnotes:
¹⁷ V.35.
¹⁸ Ibid.



### Verse 48

###### Rashi on Judges 20:48:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:48:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:48:1)


**שָׁבוּ אֶל בְּנֵי בִנְיָמִן.** בְּכָל הֶעָרִים:


**Turned to the sons of Binyomin** in all the cities.


###### Rashi on Judges 20:48:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:48:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:48:2)


**מֵעִיר מְתֹם.** הֵתַמּוּ כָּל יוֹשְׁבֵי הֶעָרִים:


**Completely annihilating the cities.** They annihilated all the inhabitants of the cities,


###### Rashi on Judges 20:48:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:48:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/20:48:3)


**וְעַד בְּהֵמָה.** וַאֲנָשִׁים בִּכְלַל ׳מְתוֹם׳ הָיוּ, שֶׁ׳מְּתוֹם׳ לְשׁוֹן כְּלָיָה הוּא וְיֵשׁ לִתְמֹהַּ עַל אֶלֶף הֵיכָן הֵם, שֶׁהֲרֵי עֶשְׂרִים וְשִׁשָּׁה אֶלֶף הִתְפַּקְּדוּ מִן הֶעָרִים, וּמִן הַגִּבְעָה שֵׁשׁ מֵאוֹת, וְכָאן מָנָה בַּנּוֹפְלִים עֶשְׂרִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה אֶלֶף וּמֵאָה אִישׁ, וְשֵׁשׁ מֵאוֹת נִמְלְטוּ אֶל סֶלַע הָרִמּוֹן, וְהָאֶלֶף בָּרְחוּ אֶל הֶעָרִים וְנָפְלוּ לְמָחֳרָת כְּשֶׁשָּׁבוּ בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל אֶל עָרֵי בִנְיָמִן לְהַחֲרִים הַנָּשִׁים וְהַטַּף:


**Including the cattle—**the people are included in "completely annihilating," since **מְּתֹם** denotes absolute annihilation. There is a problem with the location of one thousand, as twenty-six thousand were counted in the cities, and seven hundred at Givoh;¹⁹ here, those who fell totaled twenty-five thousand, one hundred troops,²⁰ and six hundred escaped to Rimon cliff.²¹ Evidently, one thousand fled to the cities, and fell the following day when the Bnei Yisroel turned to the cities of Binyomin to annihilate the women and children.

footnotes:
¹⁹ V.15.
²⁰ V. 46.
²¹ V. 47. This totals 25,700, whereas the total in v. 15 was 26,700.



## Chapter 21


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Judges 21:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/21:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/21:3:1)


**לְהִפָּקֵד.** לְהִגָּרַע:


**Will be eliminated** Removed.¹

footnotes:
¹ פקד is sometimes translated as “remembered” (Bereishis, 50:25). Here, however, the translation is “removed,” or “eliminated.”



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Judges 21:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/21:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/21:6:1)


**וַיִּנָּחֲמוּ.** שָׁבוּ לַחֲשֹׁב עֲלֵיהֶם מַחְשֶׁבֶת פְּלֵיטָה:


**Were regretful** They reversed themselves,² and contemplated escape for them.

footnotes:
² וַיִנָּחֲמוּ is translated as “they regretted,” rather than “they were comforted.”



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Judges 21:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/21:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/21:12:1)


**אַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת.** נַעֲרָה בְתוּלָה הוֹשִׁיבוּם עַל פִּי חָבִיּוֹת שֶׁל יַיִן, בְּעוּלָה רֵיחָהּ נוֹדֵף, בְּתוּלָה אֵין רֵיחָהּ נוֹדֵף:


**Four hundred virgin maids** They placed them over the openings of wine barrels. Those with carnal knowledge emitted an aroma, the virgins did not.³

footnotes:
³ Yevamos, 60:b.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Judges 21:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/21:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/21:14:1)


**וְלֹא מָצְאוּ לָהֶם.** לֹא סִפְּקוּ לָהֶם:


**This did not satisfy** They were insufficient for them.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Judges 21:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/21:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/21:17:1)


**יְרֻשַּׁת פְּלֵיטָה.** נַחֲלַת הַשֵּׁבֶט נִשְׁאֲרָה רֵיקָנִית, הָבוּ עֵצָה שֶׁיּוֹלִידוּ בָנִים וְיַחֲזִיקוּ בְּנַחֲלָתָם הַפְּלֵיטָה הַנִּשְׁאֶרֶת, וְלֹא יִמָּחֶה שֵׁבֶט מִיִּשְׂרָאֵל:


**The survivors must inherit the ancestral property** The tribe's ancestral property remains unclaimed; let us devise a plan whereby they may father children, so that the property will fall into the possession of the remaining survivors, and a tribe will not be obliterated from Yisroel.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Judges 21:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/21:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/21:19:1)


**מִזְרְחָה הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ לַמְסִלָּה.** בְּמִזְרָחָהּ שֶׁל מְסִלָּה, שִׁילֹה בִּצְפוֹנָהּ שֶׁל בֵּית אֵל וּבִדְרוֹמָהּ שֶׁל לְבוֹנָה, וּבְמִזְרָחָהּ שֶׁל מְסִלָּה הָעוֹלָה מִבֵּית אֵל שְׁכֶמָה:


**East [lit. "sunrise"] of the road.** To the east of the road. Shiloh was north of Beis El, south of Levonah, and east of the road ascending from Beis El to Shechem.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Judges 21:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/21:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/21:22:1)


**לָרִיב אֵלֵינוּ.** לָלֶכֶת אֵלֵינוּ בַּמִּלְחָמָה:


**To fight us** To go to war against us.


###### Rashi on Judges 21:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/21:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Judges/r/21:22:2)


**וְאָמַרְנוּ אֲלֵיהֶם חָנּוּנוּ אוֹתָם.** רִחַמְנוּ עֲלֵיהֶם כִּי אֵינָם יוֹדְעִים מַה לַּעֲשׂוֹת, ׳כִּי לֹא לָקַחְנוּ אִישׁ אִשְׁתּוֹ בַּמִּלְחָמָה׳ שֶׁל יָבֵשׁ גִּלְעָד אֶלָּא לְאַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת אִישׁ מֵהֶם, וְאִם עַל הַשְּׁבוּעָה אַתֶּם מַקְפִּידִים, לֹא עָוֹן לָכֶם בַּדָּבָר ׳כִּי לֹא אַתֶּם נְתַתֶּם לָהֶם׳ אֶת בְּנוֹתֵיכֶם שֶׁתְּהֵא לָכֶם אַשְׁמָה בַּדָּבָר וְשָׁלוֹם לָכֶם אַל תִּירָאוּ:


**We shall tell them we were merciful with them.** We were merciful with them because they did not know what to do, as we could not provide each of these men with a wife at the time of the war with Yoveish Gilod, except for four hundred of them. And if it is your oath that disturbs you, there is nothing sinful here on your part, since you were not the ones who provided them with your daughters, so that you would now be blameworthy. Peaceful salutations, have no fear!
